# Wikibooks processing manifest

This lists every page in the `complete-book.json` snapshot. **Unprocessed** means the page has not yet been examined for an app explanation. A redirect is identified in Notes, but remains unprocessed until reviewed. The revision link identifies the exact version in the snapshot, or the version used for an existing explanation.

Use these statuses:

- **unprocessed** — not yet examined for an explanation.
- **explained** — used for one or more app explanations; the move sequence identifies the current explanation.
- **no-useful-prose** — examined, but has no suitable explanatory prose; explain why in Notes.
- **duplicate-position** — examined, but duplicates a position already covered; identify the other sequence in Notes.
- **needs-review** — examined, but cannot yet be used confidently; explain what remains to be checked in Notes.

Update the existing row when a page is processed; add another row if it supports a distinct explanation. `—` in the move sequence column means that a sequence has not been recorded. Keep the examined revision pinned in `chess-opening-theory.json` for any explanation. When refreshing the complete snapshot, add newly discovered pages as unprocessed, retain existing processing outcomes, and record any changed revisions for review.
